\displaystyle:\equiv\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x},1_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}{x}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}},\mathsf{pullRx}_{\mathcal{B}}^{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}

Lemma 82

(Flattening of lenses of path objects onto path objects)
.

Let
\mathcal{B}
be an oplax covariant or lax contravariant lens of path objects over a path object
\mathcal{A}
. Assuming function extensionality, the flattening of
\mathcal{B}
onto
\mathcal{A}
is univalent.

Proof.

We consider only the covariant case, as the contravariant one is similar. Fixing
x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}
, we must check that the fan
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{\}}}_{\mathcal{A}_{{\downarrow}\mathcal{B}}^{+}}^{+}
is a proposition. First we compute the fan up to definitional equality:

\displaystyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{\}}}_{\mathcal{A}_{{\downarrow}\mathcal{B}}^{+}}^{+}

\displaystyle\equiv\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y:\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y:\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y:\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y:\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}_{{\downarrow}\mathcal{B}}^{+}}}y

\displaystyle\equiv\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y:\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y:\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y:\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y:\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p^{\prime}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\to\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p^{\prime}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\to\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p^{\prime}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\to\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p^{\prime}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\to\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\mathchoice{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\mathsf{push}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}{u}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}p^{\prime}u

\displaystyle\equiv\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y:\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y:\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y:\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y:\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p^{\prime}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\to\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p^{\prime}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\to\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p^{\prime}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\to\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p^{\prime}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\to\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\mathsf{push}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}\mathrel{\approx_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\pitchfork\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}p^{\prime}

\displaystyle\equiv\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y:\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y:\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y:\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y:\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{push}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\pitchfork\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}^{+}

As each
\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}
is univalent, we deduce from function extensionality that the cotensor
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\pitchfork\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}
is univalent. Therefore, each fan
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{push}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\pitchfork\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}^{+}
is contractible and so we have the following equivalences:

\displaystyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{\}}}_{\mathcal{A}_{{\downarrow}\mathcal{B}}^{+}}^{+}

\displaystyle\cong\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{push}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\pitchfork\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}^{+}

\displaystyle\cong\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y

\displaystyle\equiv\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{\}}}_{\mathcal{A}}^{+}

As
\mathcal{A}
is assumed to be univalent,
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{\}}}_{\mathcal{A}}^{+}
is a proposition and thus so is
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{\}}}_{\mathcal{A}_{{\downarrow}\mathcal{B}}^{+}}^{+}
.
∎

Construction 83

(The definitional replacement of a lens)
.

Let
\mathcal{B}
be an oplax covariant lens of reflexive graphs over a reflexive graph
\mathcal{A}
. The
covariant definitional replacement
of
\mathcal{B}
is defined to be the following
definitional
covariant lens
\mathsf{drep}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}\mathcal{B}
over the flattening
\mathcal{A}_{{\downarrow}\mathcal{B}}^{+}
with
\mathcal{B}
as its underlying family of reflexive graphs.

\displaystyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{drep}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}

\displaystyle:\equiv\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}

\displaystyle\mathsf{push}_{\mathsf{drep}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}\mathcal{B}}^{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p,p^{\prime}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}{u}

\displaystyle:\equiv p^{\prime}u

On the other hand, if
\mathcal{B}
is a lax contravariant lens of reflexive graphs over a reflexive graph
\mathcal{A}
, we define the
contravariant definitional replacement
of
\mathcal{B}
to be the following definitional contravariant lens
\mathsf{drep}^{-}_{\mathcal{A}}\mathcal{B}
over the flattening
\mathcal{A}_{{\downarrow}\mathcal{B}}^{-}
:

\displaystyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{drep}^{-}_{\mathcal{A}}\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}

\displaystyle:\equiv\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}

\displaystyle\mathsf{pull}_{\mathsf{drep}^{-}_{\mathcal{A}}\mathcal{B}}^{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p,p^{\prime}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}{u}

\displaystyle:\equiv p^{\prime}u

3.4.4
Polynomials and partial products of reflexive graphs

Any family of types
x:A\vdash B\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}:\mathbf{type}
induces a
polynomial
type operator
\mathbf{P}_{A}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle C,B$}\mathclose{}{)}}:\equiv\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:A$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:A$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:A$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:A$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\mathchoice{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u:B\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u:B\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u:B\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u:B\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}C
; the universal property of
\mathbf{P}_{A}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle C,B$}\mathclose{}{)}}
is that of a
partial product

(
Dyckhoff and Tholen, 1987
)
. In this section, we generalise the construction of polynomials to lenses of reflexive graphs; the definitions of the polynomial reflexive graphs will then mirror the description of
covariant
and
contravariant
partial products in a 2-category
(
Johnstone, 1993
;
Johnstone, 2002
)
. We then show how to specialise the construction of polynomials to obtain suitable path object structures on the
partial map classifier
of a given dominance as in synthetic domain theory
(
Rosolini, 1986
)
.

In this section, we assume function extensionality.

Construction 84
.

Let
\mathcal{B}
be a definitional covariant lens of reflexive graphs over a reflexive graph
\mathcal{A}
, and let
\mathcal{C}
be an arbitrary reflexive graph. Then we may define a
definitional contravariant
lens of reflexive graphs
\mathsf{pp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{C},\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{)}}
over
\mathcal{A}
by setting each
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{pp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{C},\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}:\equiv\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\pitchfork\mathcal{C}
. The definitional contravariant lens structure of
\mathsf{pp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{C},\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{)}}
over
\mathcal{A}
is defined from the definitional covariant lens structure of
\mathcal{B}
by precomposition like so:

x,y:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}};p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y;c:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\to\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{C}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}

\vdash\mathsf{pull}_{\mathsf{pp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{C},\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}^{p}{c}:\equiv c\circ\mathsf{push}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}

Construction 85
.

Let
\mathcal{B}
be a definitional contravariant lens of reflexive graphs over a reflexive graph
\mathcal{A}
, and let
\mathcal{C}
be an arbitrary reflexive graph. Then we may define a
definitional covariant
family of path objects
\mathsf{pp}^{-}_{\mathcal{A}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{C},\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{)}}
over
\mathcal{A}
by setting each
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{pp}^{-}_{\mathcal{A}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{C},\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}:\equiv\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\pitchfork\mathcal{C}
. The definitional covariant lens structure of
\mathsf{pp}^{-}_{\mathcal{A}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{C},\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{)}}
over
\mathcal{A}
is defined from the definitional contravariant lens struture of
\mathcal{B}
by precomposition:

x,y:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}};p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y;c:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\to\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{C}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}

\vdash\mathsf{push}_{\mathsf{pp}^{-}_{\mathcal{A}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{C},\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}^{p}{c}:\equiv c\circ\mathsf{pull}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}

Definition 86

(Partial products of definitional lenses)
.

Let
\mathcal{B}
be a definitional covariant lens of reflexive graphs over a reflexive graph
\mathcal{A}
. Then the
covariant partial product
of a reflexive graph
\mathcal{C}
with
\mathcal{B}
over
\mathcal{A}
is defined to be the following total reflexive graph:

\mathcal{P}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{C},\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{)}}:\equiv\mathcal{A}.\mathsf{disp}^{-}_{\mathcal{A}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{pp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{C},\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}

When
\mathcal{B}
is a definitional contravariant lens, we define the
contravariant partial product
of
\mathcal{C}
with
\mathcal{B}
over
\mathcal{A}
as follows:

\mathcal{P}^{-}_{\mathcal{A}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{C},\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{)}}:\equiv\mathcal{A}.\mathsf{disp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{pp}^{-}_{\mathcal{A}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{C},\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}

Computation 87

(Covariant partial product)
.

The covariant partial product of a reflexive graph
\mathcal{C}
with a definitional covariant lens of reflexive graphs
\mathcal{B}
over a reflexive graphs
\mathcal{A}
computes as follows:

\displaystyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{P}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{C},\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}

\displaystyle\equiv\mathbf{P}_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{C}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}},\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}

\displaystyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,c$}\mathclose{}{)}}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{P}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{C},\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y,d$}\mathclose{}{)}}

\displaystyle\equiv\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\mathchoice{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u:\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u:\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u:\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u:\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}cu\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{C}}}d\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{push}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}{u}$}\mathclose{}{)}}

\displaystyle\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{P}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{C},\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,c$}\mathclose{}{)}}

\displaystyle\equiv\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x},\lambda{u}\mathpunct{.}\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{C}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle cu$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}

Computation 88

(Contravariant partial product)
.

The contravariant partial product of a reflexive graph
\mathcal{C}
with a definitional contravariant lens of reflexive graphs
\mathcal{B}
over a reflexive graph
\mathcal{A}
unravels up to definitional equivlance as follows:

\displaystyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{P}^{-}_{\mathcal{A}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{C},\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}

\displaystyle\equiv\mathbf{P}_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{C}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}},\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}

\displaystyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,c$}\mathclose{}{)}}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{P}^{-}_{\mathcal{A}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{C},\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y,d$}\mathclose{}{)}}

\displaystyle\equiv\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\mathchoice{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u:\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u:\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u:\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u:\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}c\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{pull}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}{u}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{C}}}du

\displaystyle\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{P}^{-}_{\mathcal{A}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{C},\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,c$}\mathclose{}{)}}

\displaystyle\equiv\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x},\lambda{u}\mathpunct{.}\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{C}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle cu$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}

Example 89

(Partial map classifiers)
.

A
dominance
in the sense of
Rosolini (1986)
is a univalent universe
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\Sigma,T$}\mathclose{}{)}}
closed under the terminal type and internal sums such that for each
\phi:\Sigma
, the component
T\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\phi$}\mathclose{}{)}}
is a proposition. In this case, we may equip each
T\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\phi$}\mathclose{}{)}}
with the codiscrete path object structure
{\triangledown}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle T\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\phi$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}
so that
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\Sigma,{\triangledown}{T}$}\mathclose{}{)}}
is a univalent family of path objects and
{\triangledown}{T}
has definitional covariant and contravariant lens structure over the propositional reflexive graph image
\Sigma/_{-1}T
.

The partial map classifier of a given type
A
in the dominance
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\Sigma,T$}\mathclose{}{)}}
is the polynomial type
\mathbf{P}_{\Sigma}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle A,T$}\mathclose{}{)}}\equiv\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\phi:\Sigma$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\phi:\Sigma$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\phi:\Sigma$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\phi:\Sigma$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\mathchoice{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p:T\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\phi$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p:T\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\phi$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p:T\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\phi$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p:T\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\phi$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}A
. A suitable lifting of the partial map classifiers to path objects
\mathcal{A}
can be obtained by means of covariant or contravariant partial products:

\mathcal{A}_{\bot}^{+}:\equiv\mathcal{P}^{+}_{\Sigma/_{-1}T}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A},{\triangledown}{T}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\qquad\mathcal{A}_{\bot}^{-}:\equiv\mathcal{P}^{-}_{\Sigma/_{-1}T}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A},{\triangledown}{T}$}\mathclose{}{)}}

We compute the above reflexive graph structures as follows:

\displaystyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}_{\bot}^{+}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\equiv\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}_{\bot}^{-}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\equiv\mathbf{P}_{\Sigma}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}},T$}\mathclose{}{)}}

\displaystyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\phi,a$}\mathclose{}{)}}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}_{\bot}^{+}}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\psi,b$}\mathclose{}{)}}\equiv\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p,q$}\mathclose{}{)}}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\phi\to\psi$}\mathclose{}{)}}\times\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\psi\to\phi$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p,q$}\mathclose{}{)}}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\phi\to\psi$}\mathclose{}{)}}\times\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\psi\to\phi$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p,q$}\mathclose{}{)}}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\phi\to\psi$}\mathclose{}{)}}\times\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\psi\to\phi$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p,q$}\mathclose{}{)}}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\phi\to\psi$}\mathclose{}{)}}\times\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\psi\to\phi$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\mathchoice{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u:T\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\phi$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u:T\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\phi$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u:T\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\phi$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u:T\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\phi$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}au\mathrel{\approx_{A}}b\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle pu$}\mathclose{}{)}}

\displaystyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\phi,a$}\mathclose{}{)}}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}_{\bot}^{-}}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\psi,b$}\mathclose{}{)}}\equiv\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p,q$}\mathclose{}{)}}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\phi\to\psi$}\mathclose{}{)}}\times\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\psi\to\phi$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p,q$}\mathclose{}{)}}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\phi\to\psi$}\mathclose{}{)}}\times\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\psi\to\phi$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p,q$}\mathclose{}{)}}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\phi\to\psi$}\mathclose{}{)}}\times\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\psi\to\phi$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p,q$}\mathclose{}{)}}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\phi\to\psi$}\mathclose{}{)}}\times\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\psi\to\phi$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\mathchoice{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle \displaystyle\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}_{\bot}^{+}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\phi,a$}\mathclose{}{)}}\equiv\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}_{\bot}^{-}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\phi,a$}\mathclose{}{)}}\equiv\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\lambda{u}\mathpunct{.}{u},\lambda{u}\mathpunct{.}{u}$}\mathclose{}{)}},\lambda{u}\mathpunct{.}\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle au$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}

Example 90

(Lists)
.

A simple example of a polynomial type operator is that of
lists
: letting
F\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle n$}\mathclose{}{)}}
be the standard finite type
\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle i:\mathbb{N}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle i:\mathbb{N}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle i:\mathbb{N}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle i:\mathbb{N}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}i<n
, we may define
\mathsf{List}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}:\equiv\mathbf{P}_{\mathbb{N}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle A,F$}\mathclose{}{)}}
. This representation of lists lifts to path objects by means of partial products via our account in
Section

3.4.2
of the path object
\Delta_{a}
classifying finite ordinals and the definitional lens
\mathcal{F}
of elements of a given finite ordinal. In particular, for any path object
\mathcal{A}
we consider the following covariant and contravariant partial products:

\mathsf{List}^{+}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{)}}:\equiv\mathcal{P}^{+}_{\Delta_{a}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A},\mathcal{F}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\qquad\mathsf{List}^{-}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{)}}:\equiv\mathcal{P}^{-}_{\Delta_{a}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A},\mathcal{F}$}\mathclose{}{)}}

These reflexive graphs compute as follows:

\displaystyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{List}^{+}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\equiv\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{List}^{-}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\equiv\mathsf{List}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}\equiv\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle n:\mathbb{N}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle n:\mathbb{N}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle n:\mathbb{N}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle n:\mathbb{N}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\mathchoice{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle i:F\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle n$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle i:F\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle n$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle i:F\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle n$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle i:F\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle n$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}A

\displaystyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle m,a$}\mathclose{}{)}}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{List}^{+}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle n,b$}\mathclose{}{)}}\equiv\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle f,f^{\leq}$}\mathclose{}{)}}:m\mathrel{\approx_{\Delta_{a}}}n$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle f,f^{\leq}$}\mathclose{}{)}}:m\mathrel{\approx_{\Delta_{a}}}n$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle f,f^{\leq}$}\mathclose{}{)}}:m\mathrel{\approx_{\Delta_{a}}}n$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle f,f^{\leq}$}\mathclose{}{)}}:m\mathrel{\approx_{\Delta_{a}}}n$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\mathchoice{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle i:F\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle m$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle i:F\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle m$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle i:F\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle m$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle i:F\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle m$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}ai\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}b\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle fi$}\mathclose{}{)}}

\displaystyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle m,a$}\mathclose{}{)}}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{List}^{-}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle n,b$}\mathclose{}{)}}\equiv\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle f,f^{\leq}$}\mathclose{}{)}}:m\mathrel{\approx_{\Delta_{a}}}n$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle f,f^{\leq}$}\mathclose{}{)}}:m\mathrel{\approx_{\Delta_{a}}}n$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle f,f^{\leq}$}\mathclose{}{)}}:m\mathrel{\approx_{\Delta_{a}}}n$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle f,f^{\leq}$}\mathclose{}{)}}:m\mathrel{\approx_{\Delta_{a}}}n$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\mathchoice{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle i:F\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle n$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle i:F\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle n$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle i:F\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle n$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle i:F\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle n$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}a\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle fi$}\mathclose{}{)}}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}bi

\displaystyle\mathsf{rx}_{\mathsf{List}^{+}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle m,a$}\mathclose{}{)}}\equiv\mathsf{rx}_{\mathsf{List}^{-}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle m,a$}\mathclose{}{)}}\equiv\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{idnEquiv}_{F\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle m$}\mathclose{}{)}}},\mathsf{idnMono}$}\mathclose{}{)}},\lambda{i}\mathpunct{.}\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle ai$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}

Buchholtz (2023)
has shown how to use the classifying space of the symmetric group on two elements to define several variations on unordered pairs in univalent foundations. In what follows, we assume the existence of propositional truncations
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lVert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle-$}\mathclose{}{\rVert}}
.

Example 91

(Homotopy unordered pairs
(
Buchholtz, 2023
)
)
.

Let
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle U,E$}\mathclose{}{)}}
be a univalent universe containing the two-element type
\mathbf{2}
; following
Buchholtz (2023)
we may define the “classifying type”
B\Sigma_{2}
to
the type of
U
-small types merely equivalent to
\mathbf{2}
as follows,
i.e
.
we define
B\Sigma_{2}:\equiv\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle X:U$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle X:U$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle X:U$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle X:U$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lVert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{Equiv}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle E\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle X$}\mathclose{}{)}},\mathbf{2}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rVert}}
. We define a decoding family
K:B\Sigma_{2}\vdash E_{2}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle K$}\mathclose{}{)}}:\mathbf{type}
by setting

. Then
op. cit
.
defines the type of

in a type

A suitable path object structure on

is obtained by path object comprehension:

\mathcal{B}\Sigma_{2}:\equiv\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\SavedStyle X:U/E$}\mathrel{{|}}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lVert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{Equiv}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle E\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle X$}\mathclose{}{)}},\mathbf{2}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rVert}}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}

E_{2}

77

These reflexive graph structures compute as follows:

\displaystyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{hUP}^{+}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\equiv\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{hUP}^{-}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\equiv\mathsf{hUP}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}

\displaystyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle 3.5
Coherence in the theory of path object lenses

Lemma 92
.

Assuming dependent function extensionality, it is a proposition that a given reflexive graph is univalent.

Proof.

Let
\mathcal{A}
be a reflexive graph; a witness that
\mathcal{A}
is univalent is an element of the following product:

\mathchoice{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\mathsf{isProp}\,\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{\}}}_{\mathcal{A}}^{+}

As being a proposition is a proposition, this is a product of propositions. Dependent function extensionality implies the closure of propositions under products.
∎

Lemma 93
.

Let
\mathcal{A}
be a reflexive graph, and let
\mathcal{B}
be a displayed reflexive graph over
\mathcal{A}
. Assuming dependent function extensionality, it is a proposition that
\mathcal{B}
is univalent.

Proof.

Univalence of
\mathcal{B}
amounts to the following product:

\mathchoice{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\mathsf{isUnivalent}\,\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}

By dependent function extensionality, it suffices to check that each
\mathsf{isUnivalent}\,\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}
is a proposition; but this is
Lemma

92
.
∎

This co-fan is indeed a proposition, as we have assumed that each

∎

Lemma 95
.

Let
\mathcal{A}
be a path object and let
\mathcal{B}
be a family of path objects over
\mathcal{A}
. Assuming dependent function extensionality, the type of lax contravariant lens structures on
\mathcal{B}
over
\mathcal{A}
is a proposition.

Proof.

Analogous to
Lemma

94
.
∎

Lemma 96

As
\mathcal{A}
is univalent, the fan
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{\}}}_{\mathcal{A}}^{+}
contracts onto
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}
as reflected below:

{}\times\ThisStyle{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\big(}{\big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathchoice{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\Psi\,u$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\big)}{\big)}{\big)}{)}}}

The above is definitionally equivalent to the following:

\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\Phi:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\to\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\Phi:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\to\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\Phi:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\to\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\Phi:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\to\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}

\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\Psi:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\to\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\Psi:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\to\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\Psi:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\to\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\Psi:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\to\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}

{}\times\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\Phi\mathrel{\approx_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\pitchfork\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\Psi$}\mathclose{}{)}}

{}\times\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\lambda{u}\mathpunct{.}{u}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\mathrel{\approx_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\pitchfork\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\Psi$}\mathclose{}{)}}

We can re-associate the above as follows:

\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\Phi:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\to\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\Phi:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\to\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\Phi:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\to\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\Phi:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\to\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}

\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\Psi,\hat{\Psi}$}\mathclose{}{)}}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\lambda{u}\mathpunct{.}u$}\mathclose{}{\}}}_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\pitchfork{\mathcal{B}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}^{+}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\Psi,\hat{\Psi}$}\mathclose{}{)}}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\lambda{u}\mathpunct{.}u$}\mathclose{}{\}}}_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\pitchfork{\mathcal{B}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}^{+}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\Psi,\hat{\Psi}$}\mathclose{}{)}}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\lambda{u}\mathpunct{.}u$}\mathclose{}{\}}}_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\pitchfork{\mathcal{B}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}^{+}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\Psi,\hat{\Psi}$}\mathclose{}{)}}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\lambda{u}\mathpunct{.}u$}\mathclose{}{\}}}_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\pitchfork{\mathcal{B}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}^{+}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}

\Phi\mathrel{\approx_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\pitchfork\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\Psi

As
\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{a}$}\mathclose{}{)}}
is assumed to be univalent, so is its cotensor by
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}
; thus, the fan of
\lambda{u}\mathpunct{.}{u}
in the cotensor contracts to
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\lambda{u}\mathpunct{.}{u},\lambda{u}\mathpunct{.}{\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}{u}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}
:

\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\Phi:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\to\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\Phi:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\to\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\Phi:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\to\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\Phi:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\to\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}

\Phi\mathrel{\approx_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\pitchfork\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\lambda{u}\mathpunct{.}{u}

The above is just the co-fan
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\lambda{u}\mathpunct{.}u$}\mathclose{}{\}}}_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\pitchfork{\mathcal{B}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}n}^{-}
, which is a proposition because
\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,x,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}
is assumed univalent.
∎

4
Case study: path objects classifying reflexive graphs

In this section, we assume dependent function extensionality.
Let
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle U,E$}\mathclose{}{)}}
be a univalent family in the sense of
Definition

40
so that the reflexive graph image
U/E
is univalent. Given
A:U
, we will write
A:\mathbf{type}
in place of
E\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}
when it causes no confusion.

4.1
Classifying reflexive graphs

Construction 97

(A univalent lens for graph structures)
.

A
U
-small graph structure on
A:U
is given by a vertex of the path structure
\mathsf{GphOn}_{U}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}:\equiv A\pitchfork A\pitchfork U/E
. We shall equip
\mathsf{GphOn}_{U}
as a
lax contravariant lens of path objects
over
U/E
as follows:

A_{0},A_{1}:U;f:\mathsf{Equiv}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle A_{0},A_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}},E:A_{1}\to A_{1}\to U\vdash

\mathsf{push}_{\mathsf{GphOn}_{U}}^{f}{E}:A_{0}\to A_{0}\to U

\mathsf{push}_{\mathsf{GphOn}_{U}}^{f}{E}\,x\,y:\equiv E\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle fx$}\mathclose{}{)}}\,\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle fy$}\mathclose{}{)}}

A:U,E:A\to A\to U\vdash

\mathsf{pushRx}_{\mathsf{GphOn}_{U}}^{A}{E}:\mathsf{push}_{\mathsf{GphOn}_{U}}^{\mathsf{idnEquiv}_{A}}{E}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{GphOn}_{U}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}E

\mathsf{pushRx}_{\mathsf{GphOn}_{U}}^{A}{E}:\equiv\mathsf{rx}_{\mathsf{GphOn}_{U}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle A$}\mathclose{}{)}}}{E}

Computation 98

(A path object classifying graphs)
.

Thus we obtain a path object classifying
U
-small graphs by taking the total path object of the display of the lax contravariant lens
\mathsf{GphOn}_{U}
over
U/E
, defining
\mathsf{Gph}_{U}:\equiv\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle U/E$}\mathclose{}{)}}.\mathsf{disp}^{-}_{U/E}{\mathsf{GphOn}_{U}}
.
Unraveling definitions, we see that its underlying reflexive graph structure is precisely the natural one that would arise from invertible graph homomorphisms:

\displaystyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{Gph}_{U}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}

\displaystyle\equiv\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle A:U$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle A:U$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle A:U$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle A:U$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{A\to A\to U}

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{0}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{Gph}_{U}}}\mathcal{G}_{1}

\displaystyle\equiv\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle f:\mathsf{Equiv}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{G}_{0}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}},\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{G}_{1}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle f:\mathsf{Equiv}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{G}_{0}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}},\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{G}_{1}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle f:\mathsf{Equiv}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{G}_{0}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}},\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{G}_{1}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle f:\mathsf{Equiv}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{G}_{0}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}},\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{G}_{1}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\mathchoice{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,y:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{G}_{0}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,y:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{G}_{0}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,y:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{G}_{0}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,y:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{G}_{0}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\mathsf{Equiv}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{G}_{0}}}y,fx\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{G}_{1}}}fy$}\mathclose{}{)}}

\displaystyle\mathsf{rx}_{\mathsf{Gph}_{U}}{\mathcal{G}}

\displaystyle\equiv\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{idnEquiv}_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{G}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}},\lambda{x,y}\mathpunct{.}\mathsf{idnEquiv}_{x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{G}}}y}$}\mathclose{}{)}}

Given
f:\mathcal{G}_{0}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{Gph}_{U}}}\mathcal{G}_{1}
, we will write
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle f$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}:\mathsf{Equiv}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{G}_{0}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}},\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{G}_{1}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}
for the first component and
f^{\approx}:\mathchoice{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,y:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{G}_{0}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,y:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{G}_{0}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,y:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{G}_{0}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,y:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{G}_{0}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\mathsf{Equiv}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{G}_{0}}}y,\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle f$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{G}_{1}}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle f$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}y$}\mathclose{}{)}}
for the second component.

In order to classify reflexive graphs, we naturally wish to define a further lens over
\mathsf{Gph}_{U}
whose components classify reflexivity data on a given graph. Unfortunately, due to the mixed variance of reflexivity data in the underlying graph, it seems we can find neither a suitable lax contravariant nor oplax covariant lens for reflexivity data. It happens, however, that the desirable displayed path object over
\mathsf{Gph}_{U}
arises via
Lemma

65
from a particularly simple
unbiased dependent lens
that we now define straightaway.

Construction 99

(A univalent unbiased dependent lens for reflexivity data)
.

A reflexivity datum over an
equivalence
of
U
-small graphs
f:\mathcal{A}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{Gph}_{U}}}\mathcal{B}
is given by an assignment of self-edges in the image of
f
; these are the vertices of the following family of path objects:

\mathcal{A},\mathcal{B}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{Gph}_{U}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}};f:\mathcal{A}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{Gph}_{U}}}\mathcal{B}\vdash

\mathsf{RxOn}_{U}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle f$}\mathclose{}{)}}:\equiv\mathchoice{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\triangle}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle f$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle f$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}x$}\mathclose{}{)}}

When instantiated to the reflexivity datum in
\mathsf{Gph}_{U}
on a specific graph
\mathcal{A}
, we recover the usual notion of reflexivity data up to definitional equality:

\mathsf{RxOn}_{U}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{rx}_{\mathsf{Gph}_{U}}{\mathcal{A}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\equiv\mathchoice{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\triangle}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}x$}\mathclose{}{)}}

We now equip
\mathsf{RxOn}_{U}
with the structure of a unbiased dependent lens over
\mathsf{Gph}_{U}
:

\mathcal{A},\mathcal{B}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{Gph}_{U}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}};f:\mathcal{A}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{Gph}_{U}}}\mathcal{B};\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{RxOn}_{U}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{rx}_{\mathsf{Gph}_{U}}{\mathcal{A}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\vdash

\mathsf{lext}_{\mathsf{RxOn}_{U}}^{f}{\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{RxOn}_{U}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle f$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}

\mathsf{lext}_{\mathsf{RxOn}_{U}}^{f}{\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}}:\equiv\lambda{x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}}\mathpunct{.}f^{\approx}\,x\,x\,\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}

\mathcal{A},\mathcal{B}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{Gph}_{U}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}};f:\mathcal{A}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{Gph}_{U}}}\mathcal{B};\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{B}}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{RxOn}_{U}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{rx}_{\mathsf{Gph}_{U}}{\mathcal{B}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\vdash

\mathsf{rext}_{\mathsf{RxOn}_{U}}^{f}{\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{B}}}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{RxOn}_{U}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle f$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}

\mathsf{rext}_{\mathsf{RxOn}_{U}}^{f}{\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{B}}}:\equiv\lambda{x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}}\mathpunct{.}\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{B}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle f$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}x$}\mathclose{}{)}}

The laws of the dependent lens hold definitionally, so we are done!

Computation 100

(A path object classifying reflexive graphs)
.

A path object classifying
U
-small reflexive graphs is obtained from the display of the unbiased dependent lens
\mathsf{RxOn}_{U}
over
\mathsf{Gph}_{U}
by setting
\mathsf{RxGph}_{U}:\equiv\mathsf{Gph}_{U}.\mathsf{disp}^{\pm}_{\mathsf{Gph}_{U}}{\mathsf{RxOn}_{U}}
. It is not difficult to see that the underlying reflexive graph of
\mathsf{RxGph}_{U}
is precisely the desired one:

\displaystyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{RxGph}_{U}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}

\displaystyle\equiv\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{G}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{Gph}_{U}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{G}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{Gph}_{U}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{G}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{Gph}_{U}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{G}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{Gph}_{U}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\mathchoice{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{G}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{G}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{G}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{G}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{G}}}x

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{0}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{RxGph}_{U}}}\mathcal{A}_{1}

\displaystyle\equiv\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle f:\mathcal{A}_{0}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{Gph}_{U}}}\mathcal{A}_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle f:\mathcal{A}_{0}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{Gph}_{U}}}\mathcal{A}_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle f:\mathcal{A}_{0}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{Gph}_{U}}}\mathcal{A}_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle f:\mathcal{A}_{0}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{Gph}_{U}}}\mathcal{A}_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\mathchoice{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}_{0}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}_{0}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}_{0}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}_{0}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}f^{\approx}\,x\,x\,\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}_{0}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}=_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle f$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle f$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}x}\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle f$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}x$}\mathclose{}{)}}

\displaystyle\mathsf{rx}_{\mathsf{RxGph}_{U}}{\mathcal{A}}

\displaystyle\equiv\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{rx}_{\mathsf{Gph}_{U}}{\mathcal{A}},\lambda{x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}}\mathpunct{.}\mathbf{refl}$}\mathclose{}{)}}

Given

for the second component.

Classifying displayed reflexive graphs over a fixed base

In this section, let
\mathcal{A}
be a fixed reflexive graph.

Computation 104
.

From the display of
\mathsf{DRxOn}_{U}^{\mathcal{A}}
over
\mathsf{DGph}_{U}^{\mathcal{A}}
, we obtain a path object classifying displayed reflexive graphs over
\mathcal{A}
, defining
\mathsf{DRxGphOver}_{U}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{)}}
as follows:

\mathsf{DRxGphOver}_{U}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{)}}:\equiv\mathsf{DGph}_{U}^{\mathcal{A}}.\mathsf{disp}^{\pm}_{\mathsf{DGph}_{U}^{\mathcal{A}}}{\mathsf{DRxOn}_{U}^{\mathcal{A}}}

Unraveling definitions, we see that the underlying reflexive graph
\mathsf{DRxGphOver}_{U}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{)}}
is precisely the desirable one classifying displayed reflexive graphs over

:
