4.3
Classifying displayed reflexive graphs with variable base

Construction 105

(A lax contravariant lens for
U
-small displayed reflexive graphs over a given reflexive graph)
.

We can extend
\mathsf{DRxGphOver}_{U}
with the structure of a definitional contravariant lens over
\mathsf{RxGph}_{U}
. We first describe how to restrict a displayed reflexive graph
\mathcal{B}
along a reflexive graph equivalence
f:\mathcal{A}_{0}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{RxGph}_{U}}}\mathcal{A}_{1}
; on vertices and edges, one simply uses the restriction of the corresponding components of
\mathcal{B}
along
f
:

\mathcal{A}_{0},\mathcal{A}_{1}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{RxGph}_{U}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}};f:\mathcal{A}_{0}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{RxGph}_{U}}}\mathcal{A}_{1},\mathcal{B}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{DRxGphOver}_{U}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}_{1}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\vdash

\mathsf{pull}_{\mathsf{DRxGphOver}_{U}}^{f}{\mathcal{B}}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{DRxGphOver}_{U}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}_{0}$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}

\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{pull}_{\mathsf{DRxGphOver}_{U}}^{f}{\mathcal{B}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}x:\equiv\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle fx$}\mathclose{}{)}}

u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{pull}_{\mathsf{DRxGphOver}_{U}}^{f}{\mathcal{B}}}^{p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y}}v:\equiv{u}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}}^{f^{\approx}xyp}}{v}

To define the restricted reflexivity datum, we must transport along the witness that
f
preserves reflexivity data.

\mathsf{rx}_{\mathsf{pull}_{\mathsf{DRxGphOver}_{U}}^{f}{\mathcal{B}}}^{x}{u}:u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}}^{f^{\approx}\,x\,x\,\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}u

\mathsf{rx}_{\mathsf{pull}_{\mathsf{DRxGphOver}_{U}}^{f}{\mathcal{B}}}^{x}{u}:\equiv\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle f^{\mathsf{rx}}x$}\mathclose{}{)}}_{*}^{u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}}^{\bullet}}u}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{\mathchoice{\big(}{\big(}{\big(}{(}}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{B}}^{fx}{u}$}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\big)}{\big)}{\big)}{)}}}

The lens is definitional because
\mathcal{B}\equiv\mathsf{pull}_{\mathsf{DRxGphOver}_{U}}^{\mathsf{rx}_{\mathsf{RxGph}_{U}}{\mathcal{A}}}\mathcal{B}
definitionally.

Computation 106

(A path object classifying
U
-small displayed reflexive graphs over a variable base)
.

By computing the display of
\mathsf{DRxGphOver}_{U}
over
\mathsf{RxGph}_{U}
, we obtain a path object classifying reflexive graphs
\mathcal{A}
equipped with a displayed reflexive graph
\mathcal{B}
over
\mathcal{A}
.

\mathsf{DRxGph}_{U}:\equiv\mathsf{RxGph}_{U}.\mathsf{disp}^{-}_{\mathsf{RxGph}_{U}}{\mathsf{DRxGphOver}_{U}}

4.4
Classifying families and lenses of reflexive graphs

Construction 107

(A definitional covariant lens for vertices)
.

We can define a definitional covariant lens
\mathsf{Vtx}_{U}
over the path object
\mathsf{RxGph}_{U}
whose component over a
U
-small reflexive graph
\mathcal{A}
is
\mathcal{A}
itself. Setting
\mathsf{Vtx}_{U}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{)}}:\equiv\mathcal{A}
, we define the definitional lens structure as follows:

\mathcal{A}_{0},\mathcal{A}_{1}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{RxGph}_{U}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}};f:\mathcal{A}_{0}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{RxGph}_{U}}}\mathcal{A}_{1};x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}

\vdash\mathsf{push}_{\mathsf{Vtx}_{U}}^{f}{x}:\equiv\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle f$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}x

Computation 108

(A path object classifying
U
-small families of
U
-small reflexive graphs)
.

Using our theory of partial products of lenses (
Section

3.4.4
), we can define a path object that classifies pairs
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A},\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{)}}
where
\mathcal{A}
is a
U
-small reflexive graph and
\mathcal{B}
is a family of
U
-small reflexive graphs indexed in the vertices of

.

\mathsf{RxGphFam}_{U}:\equiv\mathcal{P}^{+}_{\mathsf{RxGph}_{U}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{RxGph}_{U},\mathsf{Vtx}_{U}$}\mathclose{}{)}}

We have the following computation:

\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{RxGphFam}_{U}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\equiv\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{RxGph}_{U}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{RxGph}_{U}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{RxGph}_{U}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{RxGph}_{U}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\mathchoice{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{RxGph}_{U}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}

\mathsf{rx}_{\mathsf{RxGphFam}_{U}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A},\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\equiv\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{rx}_{\mathsf{RxGph}_{U}}{\mathcal{A}},\lambda{x}\mathpunct{.}\mathsf{rx}_{\mathsf{RxGph}_{U}}{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}

It would be desirable to find a good path object classifying lens structures on a given family of reflexive graphs. Unfortunately, there are some limitations on our ability to do this: although the pushforward/pullback data are easy to classify, it is unclear how to define a notion of path between oplax/lax unitors
over
a homotopy between pushforward/pullback data. Therefore, the best we can do is impose the
discrete
reflexive graph structure on the first component of a lens, combining it with a family of path object structures classifying unitors by coproduct (
Definitions

19
and
49
); on the other hand, we can still exhibit a useful displayed reflexive graph structure classifying lenses with variable base by means of a unbiased dependent lens as we shall see in
Construction

109
below.

Computation 110

(A path object classifying oplax covariant lenses with fixed base)
.

Finally, we may define path object classifying oplax covariant lenses over a fixed reflexive graph
\mathcal{A}
.

\mathsf{Lens}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}:\equiv\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\pitchfork\mathsf{RxGph}_{U}$}\mathclose{}{)}}.\mathsf{disp}^{\pm}_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\pitchfork\mathsf{RxGph}_{U}}{\mathsf{LensStr}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}}

5
Fibred reflexive graphs

A lens is kind of like a fibration in which the lifts have no universal property, as noted by
Chollet et al. (2022)
. In this section, we make the analogy between lenses and fibrations of reflexive graphs precise by characterising fibrations
precisely
as lenses of path objects in
Corollary

129
.
We begin by translating the notion of
fibred
reflexive graphs from
Rijke (2019)
into the language of
displayed
reflexive graphs.

Definition 111
.

A
covariant fibration
over a reflexive graph
\mathcal{A}
is defined to be a displayed reflexive graph
\mathcal{B}
over
\mathcal{A}
such that for every edge
p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y
and displayed vertex
u:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}
, the sum
\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle v:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle v:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle v:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle v:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}}v
is contractible.

We will write
p_{*}^{\mathcal{B}}u:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}
and
p_{\dagger}^{\mathcal{B}}u:u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}}p_{*}^{\mathcal{B}}u
for the first and second components component of the centre of contraction respectively.

Definition 112
.

A
contravariant fibration
over a reflexive graph
\mathcal{A}
is defined to be a displayed reflexive graph
\mathcal{B}
over
\mathcal{A}
such that for every edge
p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y
and displayed vertex
v:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}
, the sum
\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle v^{\prime}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle v^{\prime}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle v^{\prime}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle v^{\prime}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}v^{\prime}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}}v
is contractible.

We shall write
p^{*}_{\mathcal{B}}v:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}
and
p^{\dagger}_{\mathcal{B}}v:p^{*}_{\mathcal{B}}v\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}}v
for the first and second components of the centre of contraction respectively.

5.1
Duality involution for fibred reflexive graphs

In this section, we show that covariant and contravariant fibrations are interchangeable via the duality involution for displayed reflexive graphs (
Section

2.2
).

Lemma 113

(Total opposite of a fibration)
.

Let
\mathcal{B}
be a covariant (
resp
.
contravariant) fibration of reflexive graphs over a reflexive graph
\mathcal{A}
. Then the total opposite displayed reflexive graph
\mathcal{B}^{\widetilde{\mathsf{op}}}
is a contravariant (
resp
.
covariant) fibration over
\mathcal{A}^{\mathsf{op}}
.

Lemma

113
allows us to translate results about all covariant fibrations into results about all contravariant fibrations.

5.2
Fibred reflexive graphs and univalence

Fibred reflexive graphs are always univalent, as we see in
Lemma

114
. Therefore, it is natural to refer to a fibration of reflexive graphs as a fibration of path objects.

Lemma 114

(Fibrations of reflexive graphs are univalent)
.

Any (covariant, contravariant) fibration of reflexive graphs over a reflexive graph
\mathcal{A}
is univalent,
i.e
.
a displayed path object.

Proof.

Suppose without loss of generality (by
Lemma

113
) that
\mathcal{B}
is a covariant fibration. Fixing
x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}
and
u:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}
, it suffices to show that the fan
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u$}\mathclose{}{\}}}_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}}^{+}
is a proposition. We have the following definitional computation of the fan:

\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u$}\mathclose{}{\}}}_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}}^{+}

by
Definition

30

{}\equiv\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}w

by
Definition

12

{}\equiv\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}}^{\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}}}w

The latter is contractible by our assumption that
\mathcal{B}
is a covariant fibration. Suppose, on the other hand, that
\mathcal{B}
is a contravariant fibration. To show that
\mathcal{B}
is univalent, it suffices to show that each of the co-fans
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u$}\mathclose{}{\}}}_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}}^{-}
is a proposition, which we compute below:

\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u$}\mathclose{}{\}}}_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}}^{-}

by
Definition

30

{}\equiv\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}w\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}u

by
Definition

12

{}\equiv\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}w\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}}^{\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}}}u

The latter is contractible because
\mathcal{B}
is a contravariant assumption.
∎

A converse to
Lemma

114
cannot hold, as we see in
Example

115
.

Example 115
.

Not every univalent displayed reflexive graph is a fibration.
In particular, consider the following displayed reflexive graph over the codiscrete reflexive graph
{\triangledown}{\mathbf{2}}
:

i:\mathbf{2}\vdash\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{G}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle i$}\mathclose{}{)}}:\equiv i=_{\mathbf{2}}1

i,j:\mathbf{2};p:i=_{\mathbf{2}}1,q:i=_{\mathbf{2}}1\vdash p\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{G}}^{*}}q:\equiv\mathbf{1}

i:\mathbf{2},p:i=_{\mathbf{2}}1\vdash\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{G}}^{i}{p}:\equiv*

We can see that for each
i:\mathbf{2}
, the component
\mathcal{G}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle i$}\mathclose{}{)}}
is the codiscrete reflexive graph
{\triangledown}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle i=_{\mathbf{2}}1$}\mathclose{}{)}}
, which is univalent by
Lemma

52
as
\mathbf{2}
is a set. Therefore
\mathcal{G}
is univalent, and we will see that it can be neither a covariant nor a contravariant fibration.

Suppose that
\mathcal{G}
were a covariant fibration. We have an edge
{*}:1\mathrel{\approx_{{\triangledown}{\mathbf{2}}}}0
and thus a pushforward map
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle*$}\mathclose{}{)}}_{*}\colon 1=_{\mathbf{2}}1\to 0=_{\mathbf{2}}1
. On the other hand, if
\mathcal{G}
were a contravariant fibration, we would use pullback along
{*}:0\mathrel{\approx_{{\triangledown}{\mathbf{2}}}}1
to obtain a contradiction.

Example

115
also shows that not every univalent displayed reflexive graph can be obtained from a lens structure on its components.

5.3
Fibred reflexive graphs from lenses of path objects

We will now show that when a lens is valued in path objects, its display is a fibration.

Definition 116

(Universal pushforwards/pullbacks)
.

An oplax covariant lens
\mathcal{B}
over a reflexive graph
\mathcal{A}
is said to have
universal pullforwards
when for every edge
p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y
and vertex
u:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}
, the fan
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{push}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}{u}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}^{+}
is a proposition.

Conversely, a lax contravariant lens
\mathcal{B}
over a reflexive graph
\mathcal{A}
is said to have
universal pullbacks
when for every edge
p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y
and vertex
v:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}
, the co-fan
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{pull}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}{u}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}}^{-}
is a proposition.

Lemma 117

(Covariant fibrations from universal pushforwards)
.

Let
\mathcal{B}
be an oplax covariant lens over a reflexive graph
\mathcal{A}
that has universal pushforwards. Then the covariant display
\mathsf{disp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}{\mathcal{B}}
can be made into a covariant fibration of reflexive graphs in which
p_{*}u\equiv\mathsf{push}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}u
and
p_{\dagger}u\equiv\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{push}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}{u}$}\mathclose{}{)}}
.

Proof.

We note that for any edge
p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y
and vertex
u:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}
, we have the following sequence of definitional equivalences:

\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u^{\prime}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{disp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}{\mathcal{B}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u^{\prime}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{disp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}{\mathcal{B}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u^{\prime}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{disp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}{\mathcal{B}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u^{\prime}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{disp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}{\mathcal{B}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{disp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}{\mathcal{B}}}^{p}}u^{\prime}

by
Definition

56

{}\equiv\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u^{\prime}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u^{\prime}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u^{\prime}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u^{\prime}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\mathsf{push}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}{u}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}u^{\prime}

by
Definition

30

{}\equiv\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{push}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}{u}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}^{+}

Thus, we may choose a centre of contraction for the latter freely so long as it is a proposition (which we have by assumption).
∎

Lemma 118

(Contravariant fibrations from universal pullbacks)
.

Let
\mathcal{B}
be a lax contravariant lens over a reflexive graph
\mathcal{A}
that has universal pullbacks. Then the contravariant display
\mathsf{disp}^{-}_{\mathcal{A}}{\mathcal{B}}
can be made into a contravariant fibration of reflexive graphs in which
p^{*}u\equiv\mathsf{pull}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}{u}
and
p^{\dagger}u\equiv\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{pull}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}{u}$}\mathclose{}{)}}
.

Proof.

Analogous to
Lemma

117
.
∎

Corollary 119
.

An (oplax covariant, lax contravariant) lens
\mathcal{B}
has universal (pushforwards, pullbacks) if and only if each component
\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}
is univalent.

Proof.

Let
\mathcal{B}
be an oplax covariant lens over a reflexive graph
\mathcal{A}
.

1.

Suppose that
\mathcal{B}
has universal pushforwards. We want to show that each fan
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u$}\mathclose{}{\}}}_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}}^{+}
is a proposition; using our assumption of universal pushforwards, we can show that
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u$}\mathclose{}{\}}}_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}}^{+}
is a retract of
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{push}_{\mathcal{B}}^{\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}}{u}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}}^{+}
, which is a proposition because
\mathcal{B}
has universal pushforwards.

2.

Suppose conversely that each component
\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}
is univalent; then
all
fans in the components of
\mathcal{B}
are propositions, including
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\{}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{push}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}{u}$}\mathclose{}{\}}}_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}^{+}
for some
p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y
and
u:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}
.

The case for when
\mathcal{B}
is a lax contravariant lens is established by duality via
Lemmas

114
and
118
.
∎

5.4
The underlying lens of a covariant fibration of reflexive graphs

The pushforward operation
p_{*}^{\mathcal{B}}
of a covariant fibration gives rise to an oplax covariant lens structure on the components of
\mathcal{B}
, which we construct and study in this section.

1.

We first exhibit a “straightening/unstraightening” equivalence between displayed edges
u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}}^{p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y}}v
and vertical edges
\mathsf{push}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}{u}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}v
in
120
,
121
and
122
.

2.

We use the straightening equivalence to exhibit the underlying lens of a displayed reflexive graph in
Construction

123
.

3.

Finally,
Theorem

128
exhibits an equivalence of displayed reflexive graphs between any displayed reflexive graph
\mathcal{B}
and the display of its underlying lens.

5.4.1
Straightening of displayed edges

Construction 120

(Straightening of edges)
.

Let
\mathcal{B}
be a covariant fibration of reflexive graphs over a reflexive graph
\mathcal{A}
. We can define a “straightening” function that converts displayed edges in
\mathcal{B}
to vertical edges using the pushforward operation of the fibration.

First, we define a more general function incorporating a witness of contraction.

x,y:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}};p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y;u:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}};v:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}};q:u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}}v;

H:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p_{*}^{\mathcal{B}}u,p_{\dagger}^{\mathcal{B}}u$}\mathclose{}{)}}=_{\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}}w}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle v,q$}\mathclose{}{)}}

\vdash\mathsf{strGen}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}\,q\,H:p_{*}^{\mathcal{B}}u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}v

by indentification induction on
H

x,y:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}};p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y\vdash\mathsf{strGen}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}\,\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p_{\dagger}^{\mathcal{B}}u$}\mathclose{}{)}}\,\mathbf{refl}:p_{*}^{\mathcal{B}}u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}p_{*}^{\mathcal{B}}u

by reflexivity data

x,y:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}};p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y\vdash\mathsf{strGen}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}\,\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p_{\dagger}^{\mathcal{B}}u$}\mathclose{}{)}}\,\mathbf{refl}:\equiv\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{B}}^{y}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p_{*}^{\mathcal{B}}u$}\mathclose{}{)}}

Then we define the straightening function by instantiation with one of the witnesses of contraction of
\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}}w
, which we leave nameless.

x,y:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}};p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y;u:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}};v:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}};q:u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}}v\vdash

\mathsf{str}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}\,q:p_{*}^{\mathcal{B}}u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}v

\mathsf{str}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}\,q:\equiv\mathsf{strGen}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}\,q\,\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ldots$}\mathclose{}{)}}

Construction 121

(Unstraightening of edges)
.

Let
\mathcal{B}
be a covariant fibration of reflexive graphs over a reflexive graph
\mathcal{A}
. We can “unstraighten” vertical edges of the form
p_{*}^{\mathcal{B}}u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}v
to displayed edges
u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}}v
as follows:

x,y:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}};p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y,u:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}},v:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}},q:p_{*}^{\mathcal{B}}u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}v,

H:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p_{*}^{\mathcal{B}}u,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{B}}^{y}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p_{*}^{\mathcal{B}}u$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}=_{\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}p_{*}^{\mathcal{B}}u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}w}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle v,q$}\mathclose{}{)}}

\vdash\mathsf{unstrGen}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}\,q\,H:u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}}v

by indentification induction on
H

x,y:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}};p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y,u:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}

\vdash\mathsf{unstrGen}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}\,\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{B}}^{y}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p_{*}^{\mathcal{B}}u$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\,\mathbf{refl}:u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}}p_{*}^{\mathcal{B}}u

\vdash\mathsf{unstrGen}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}\,\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{B}}^{y}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p_{*}^{\mathcal{B}}u$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}\,\mathbf{refl}:\equiv p_{\dagger}^{\mathcal{B}}u

Then we define the unstraightening function by instantiation with an anonymous witness that can be obtained from the fact that
\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}p_{*}^{\mathcal{B}}u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}w
is a proposition.

x,y:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}};p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y,u:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}},v:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}};q:p_{*}^{\mathcal{B}}u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}v\vdash

\mathsf{unstr}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}\,q:u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}}v

\mathsf{unstr}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}\,q:\equiv\mathsf{unstrGen}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}\,q\,\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ldots$}\mathclose{}{)}}

Lemma 122

(Straightening is an equivalence)
.

Let
\mathcal{B}
be a covariant fibration of reflexive graphs over a reflexive graph
\mathcal{A}
. Each straightening function
\mathsf{str}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}:u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}}v\to p_{*}^{\mathcal{B}}u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}v
is an equivalence, with the inverse tracked by
\mathsf{unstr}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}:p_{*}^{\mathcal{B}}u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}v\to u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}}v
.

Proof.

We first check that straightening is a retraction of unstraightening. It suffices to prove the following slightly generalised lemma.

x,y:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}};p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y,u:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}},v:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}},q:p_{*}^{\mathcal{B}}u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}v,

H:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p_{*}^{\mathcal{B}}u,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{B}}^{y}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p_{*}^{\mathcal{B}}u$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}=_{\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}p_{*}^{\mathcal{B}}u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}w}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle v,q$}\mathclose{}{)}},

I:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p_{*}^{\mathcal{B}}u,p_{\dagger}^{\mathcal{B}}u$}\mathclose{}{)}}=_{\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}}w}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle v,\mathsf{unstrGen}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}\,q\,H$}\mathclose{}{)}}

by identification induction on
H

x,y:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}};p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y,u:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}},v:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}},

Conversely, we check that straightening is a section of unstraightening by means of the following generalised lemma.

x,y:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}};p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y,u:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}},v:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}},q:u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}}v,

I:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p_{*}^{\mathcal{B}}u,\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{B}}^{y}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle p_{*}^{\mathcal{B}}u$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}=_{\mathchoice{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\sum_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle w:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}p_{*}^{\mathcal{B}}u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}}^{\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{y}}}w}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle v,\mathsf{strGen}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}\,q\,H$}\mathclose{}{)}}

by identification induction on
H

x,y:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}};p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y,u:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}

by reflexivity

∎

5.4.2
The underlying lens of a covariant fibration

We are now equipped to construct the underlying lens of a given covariant fibration.

Construction 123

(The underlying lens of a covariant fibration)
.

A covariant fibration of reflexive graphs
\mathcal{B}
over a reflexive graph
\mathcal{A}
induces a (canonical, as we shall see) oplax covariant lens structure on the diagonal family
\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{rx}}
via straightening.

x,y:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}};p:x\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{A}}}y,u:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}\vdash

\mathsf{push}_{\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{rx}}}^{p}{u}:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}

\mathsf{push}_{\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{rx}}}^{p}{u}:\equiv p_{*}^{\mathcal{B}}u

x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}},u:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}\vdash

\mathsf{pushRx}_{\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{rx}}}^{x}u:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}$}\mathclose{}{)}}_{*}^{\mathcal{B}}u\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}u

\mathsf{pushRx}_{\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{rx}}}^{x}u:\equiv\mathsf{str}_{\mathcal{B}}^{\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{B}}^{x}{u}$}\mathclose{}{)}}

Theorem

124
below shows that if we take the display of the underlying lens of a covariant fibration, we obtain the original displayed reflexive graph.

Theorem 124

(Roundtrip for fibrations of reflexive graphs)
.

Let
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle U,E$}\mathclose{}{)}}
be a univalent universe, and let
\mathcal{B}
be a
U
-small covariant fibration of reflexive graphs over a reflexive graph
\mathcal{A}
.
Then the display
\mathsf{disp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}{\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{rx}}}
of the underlying lens of
\mathcal{B}
may be identified with
\mathcal{B}
by means of an equivalence of displayed reflexive graphs
\mathcal{B}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{DRxGphOver}_{U}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\mathsf{disp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}{\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{rx}}}
.

Proof.

We shall use the characterisation of identifications of displayed reflexive graphs that we established in
Section

4.2
. In particular, we shall construct an equivalence of displayed reflexive graphs
\phi:\mathcal{B}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{DRxGphOver}_{U}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\mathsf{disp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}{\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{rx}}}
. We can use the identity equivalence on vertices, and the straightening–unstraightening equivalence on edges:

\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\phi$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}:\mathchoice{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\scriptscriptstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\mathsf{Equiv}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}},\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{rx}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}

\phi^{\approx}:\mathchoice{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x,y:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}{\textstyle\prod_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle u:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle by introduction

5.5
Characterisation of fibred reflexive graphs

Let
\mathcal{B}
be an oplax covariant lens of
U
-small path objects over a reflexive graph
\mathcal{A}
. By
Lemma

117
, the display
\mathsf{disp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}{\mathcal{B}}
is a covariant fibration. By
Construction

123
, we obtain an oplax covariant lens
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{disp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}{\mathcal{B}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}_{\mathsf{rx}}
that we may compare with
\mathcal{B}
. We first recall from
Computation

58
the underlying reflexive graph of each component
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{disp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}{\mathcal{B}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}_{\mathsf{rx}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}
:

\displaystyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{disp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}{\mathcal{B}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}_{\mathsf{rx}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}

\displaystyle\equiv\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}

\displaystyle u\mathrel{\approx_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{disp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}{\mathcal{B}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}_{\mathsf{rx}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}v

\displaystyle\equiv\mathsf{push}_{\mathcal{B}}^{\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}}{u}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}v

\displaystyle\mathsf{rx}_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{disp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}{\mathcal{B}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}_{\mathsf{rx}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}}{u}

\displaystyle\equiv\mathsf{pushRx}_{\mathcal{B}}^{x}{u}

Computation 125
.

We compute the lens structure of
\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{disp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}{\mathcal{B}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}_{\mathsf{rx}}
as follows, recalling
123
and
117
.

\displaystyle\mathsf{push}_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{disp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}{\mathcal{B}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}_{\mathsf{rx}}}^{p}{u}

\displaystyle\equiv p_{*}^{\mathsf{disp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}{\mathcal{B}}}u\equiv\mathsf{push}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}{u}

\displaystyle\mathsf{pushRx}_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{disp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}{\mathcal{B}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}_{\mathsf{rx}}}^{x}{u}

\displaystyle\equiv\mathsf{str}_{\mathsf{disp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}{\mathcal{B}}}^{\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}}{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{rx}_{\mathsf{disp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}{\mathcal{B}}}^{x}{u}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}\equiv\mathsf{str}_{\mathsf{disp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}{\mathcal{B}}}^{\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}}{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{pushRx}_{\mathcal{B}}^{x}{u}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}

.

We have an equivalence
\eta_{\mathcal{B}}:\mathcal{B}\mathrel{\approx_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\pitchfork\mathsf{RxGph}_{U}}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{disp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}{\mathcal{B}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}_{\mathsf{rx}}
of underlying families of reflexive graphs over
\mathcal{A}

and

\displaystyle\eta_{\mathcal{B}}

.

for the extension of the lens structure from

\displaystyle\mathsf{push}_{\mathsf{lext}_{\mathsf{LensStr}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}}^{\eta_{\mathcal{B}}}\mathcal{B}}^{p}{u}

Theorem 128

(Roundtrip for oplax covariant lenses of path objects)
.

The equivalence
\eta_{\mathcal{B}}:\mathcal{B}\mathrel{\approx_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\pitchfork\mathsf{RxGph}_{U}}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{disp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}{\mathcal{B}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}_{\mathsf{rx}}
of families of reflexive graphs extends to an equivalence
\mathcal{B}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{Lens}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{disp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}{\mathcal{B}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}_{\mathsf{rx}}
of oplax covariant lenses over
\mathcal{A}
.

Proof.

We must construct a displayed equivalence
\mathcal{B}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{disp}^{\pm}_{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}\pitchfork\mathsf{RxGph}_{U}}{\mathsf{LensStr}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}}}^{\eta_{\mathcal{B}}}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{disp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}{\mathcal{B}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}_{\mathsf{rx}}
of lens structures over
\eta_{\mathcal{B}}
. This consists of an (ordinary) equivalence of lenses as below:

\mathsf{lext}_{\mathsf{LensStr}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}}^{\eta_{\mathcal{B}}}\mathcal{B}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{LensStr}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\eta_{\mathcal{B}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}\mathsf{rext}_{\mathsf{LensStr}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}}^{\eta_{\mathcal{B}}}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{disp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}{\mathcal{B}}$}\mathclose{}{)}}_{\mathsf{rx}}

Unfolding the definition of
\mathsf{Lens}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}{\eta_{\mathcal{B}}}
from
Construction

109
and using
Computation

127
, we see that the pushforward datum for each lens depicted above is definitionally equal to that of
\mathcal{B}
. Therefore, it suffices to construct an equivalence between oplax unitors as below:

x:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}},u:\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{\lvert}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle x$}\mathclose{}{)}}$}\mathclose{}{\rvert}}

\vdash\mathsf{pushRx}_{\mathcal{B}}^{y}{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{push}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}{u}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\raisebox{2.15277pt}{$\displaystyle\centerdot$}}{\raisebox{2.15277pt}{$\centerdot$}}{\raisebox{1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\,\centerdot\,$}}{\raisebox{0.43057pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\,\centerdot\,$}}}_{\mathcal{B}}q=_{\mathsf{push}_{\mathcal{B}}^{\mathsf{rx}_{\mathcal{A}}{x}}{\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathsf{push}_{\mathcal{B}}^{p}{u}$}\mathclose{}{)}}}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathcal{B}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle y$}\mathclose{}{)}}}}v}\mathsf{strGen}_{\mathsf{disp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}{\mathcal{B}}}^{p}{q}\,H

Corollary 129

(Characterisation of fibred reflexive graphs)
.

Let
\mathcal{A}
be a reflexive graph, and let
(U,E)
be a univalent universe. Assuming function extensionality, the following two types are equivalent:

1.

The type of oplax covariant (
resp
.
lax contravariant) lenses of
U
-small path objects over
\mathcal{A}
.

2.

The type of covariant (
resp
.
contravariant) fibrations of
U
-small reflexive graphs over
\mathcal{A}
.

Proof.

An oplax covariant lens of path objects
\mathcal{B}
over
\mathcal{A}
is sent to covariant fibration
\mathsf{disp}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}{\mathcal{B}}
as described in
Lemma

117
. Conversely, a covariant fibration
\mathcal{B}
over
\mathcal{A}
is sent to its underlying lens of path objects
\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{rx}}
as specified in
Construction

123
. That these transformations are mutually inverse follows from
124
and
128
, as both
\mathsf{Lens}^{+}_{\mathcal{A}}
and
\mathsf{DRxGphOver}_{U}\ThisStyle{\mathopen{}{(}\hbox{$\SavedStyle\mathcal{A}$}\mathclose{}{)}}
are univalent in the presence of function extensionality.

The equivalence between lax contravariant lenses and contravariant fibrations is established from the above by duality (
Sections

2.2
and
5.1
).
∎
